1. **Examine the two black nodes:** The black nodes are H and R.
2. **Examine the immediate neighboring nodes:**
    - The immediate neighboring nodes of H are B, G, I, and T.
    - The immediate neighboring nodes of R are C, J, and O.
3. **No overlap in the immediate neighboring nodes, so we continue to explore further neighbors:**
    - The neighbors of B are E, F, S, and T.
    - The neighbors of G are C, N, B.
    - The neighbors of I are J, Q, H.
    - The neighbors of T are B and F.
4. **Further examine the neighbors of R's neighbors:**
    - The neighbors of C are B, G, N, R.
    - The neighbors of J are I, Q, R.
    - The neighbors of O are R and Q.
5. **Look for paths that connect the neighboring nodes:**
   - From H: H → I → J → R
   - H → I → Q → O → R
   - H → G → C → R
   - From R: R → C → G → H

6. **Evaluate the found paths:**
 - Path: [H, I, J, R] has a length of 3.
 - Path: [H, I, Q, O, R] has a length of 4.
 - Path: [H, G, C, R] has a length of 3.
 - Path: [R, C, G, H] is not a valid candidates since it considers H as starting.

So the valid shortest paths are of length 3.

Length: 3, Path: [H, G, C, R]

1. **Identify the black nodes:**
   - The two black nodes are A and T.

2. **Identify immediate neighboring nodes:**
   - Node A is connected to E, B, and S.
   - Node T is connected to B, F, and H.

     * Path from A to B to T.
     * Path from A to S to C to G to H to T.

     * Edge (A, B) to immediately disconnect A.
     * Edge (T, B) to immediately disconnect T.
